- <a id="change-719436237674700"></a>**[hotAxleBoxDetectorDirection](http://data.europa.eu/949/hotAxleBoxDetectorDirection)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436237674700)
  - Added annotation on [era:hotAxleBoxDetectorDirection](http://data.europa.eu/949/hotAxleBoxDetectorDirection): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Specific for the French Italian and Swedish networks. Applicable if trackside HABD is not TSI compliant, direction of measurement of trackside Hot Axle Box Detector. If the direction of measurement is: —	the same as the direction defined by the start and end of the SoL: (N) —	the opposite to the direction defined by the start and end of the SoL: (O) —	both directions: (B)."@en
- <a id="change-719436237751500"></a>**[hotAxleBoxDetectorDirection](http://data.europa.eu/949/hotAxleBoxDetectorDirection)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436237751500)

- <a id="change-719436237825800"></a>**[hotAxleBoxDetectorDirection](http://data.europa.eu/949/hotAxleBoxDetectorDirection)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436237825800)

As its values are a list of three predefined values, it is now a SKOS."@en
- <a id="change-719436237874900"></a>**[hotAxleBoxDetectorDirection](http://data.europa.eu/949/hotAxleBoxDetectorDirection)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436237874900)

  - Added annotation on [era:hotAxleBoxDetectorIdentification](http://data.europa.eu/949/hotAxleBoxDetectorIdentification): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Specific for the French, Italian and Swedish networks. Applicable if trackside HABD is not TSI compliant, identification of trackside hot axle box detector."@en
- <a id="change-719436286344500"></a>**[dcterms:identifier](http://purl.org/dc/terms/identifier)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436286344500)
  - Added annotation on [dcterms:identifier](http://purl.org/dc/terms/identifier): [dcterms:description](http://purl.org/dc/terms/description) = "Recommended practice is to identify the resource by means of a string conforming to an identification system. Examples include International Standard Book Number (ISBN), Digital Object Identifier (DOI), and Uniform Resource Name (URN).  Persistent identifiers should be provided as HTTP URIs."@en
- <a id="change-719436286385000"></a>**[dcterms:identifier](http://purl.org/dc/terms/identifier)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436286385000)
  - Added annotation on [dcterms:identifier](http://purl.org/dc/terms/identifier): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "An unambiguous reference to the resource within a given context."@en
- <a id="change-719436286433900"></a>**[dcterms:identifier](http://purl.org/dc/terms/identifier)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436286433900)
  - Added annotation on [dcterms:identifier](http://purl.org/dc/terms/identifier): [rdfs:isDefinedBy](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#isDefinedBy) = "http://purl.org/dc/terms/"
- <a id="change-719436286452900"></a>**[dcterms:identifier](http://purl.org/dc/terms/identifier)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436286452900)
  - Added annotation on [dcterms:identifier](http://purl.org/dc/terms/identifier): [rdfs:label](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label) = "Identifier"@en
- <a id="change-719436286523200"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436286523200)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Código o identificador, como por ejemplo el CIF de una empresa, que permite identificar de forma inequívoca a una organización. Existen muchos códigos de identificación tanto nacionales como internacionales. Esta ontología no obliga al uso de ningún esquema en concreto. Los códigos de identificación utilizados en cada caso se deberían indicar mediante el uso de la propiedad “datatype” del valor del identificador. El uso de la propiedad “datatype” para especificar el esquema de notación utilizado está en consonancia con las buenas prácticas recomendadas para el uso de la propiedad \`[skos:notation](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#notation)\`, de la que esta propiedad es una especialización."@es
- <a id="change-719436286619000"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436286619000)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier): [rdfs:label](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label) = "identificatore"@it
- <a id="change-719436286644200"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436286644200)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Donne un identifiant, comme par exemple le numéro d'enregistrement d'une entreprise, qui peut être utilisé comme identifiant unique pour l'Organisation. De nombreux schémas nationaux et internationaux sont disponibles. Cette ontologie reste neutre par rapport au schéma utilisé. Le schéma particulier utilisé devrait être indiqué par le \`datatype\` de la valeur de l'identifiant. Utiliser les datatypes pour distinguer les schémas de notation est cohérent avec les bonnes pratiques pour \`[skos:notation](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#notation)\` dont cette propriété est une spécialisation."@fr
- <a id="change-719436286668700"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436286668700)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "組織を一意に識別するために使用できる会社登録番号などの識別子を与えます。"@ja
- <a id="change-719436286697300"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436286697300)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier): [rdfs:label](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label) = "identifier"@en
- <a id="change-719436286722800"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436286722800)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Indica un identificatore univoco per l'organizzazione, come ad esempio la partita IVA di un'azienda. Molti schemi di identificazione a livello nazionale e internazionale sono disponibili allo scopo. L'ontologia ORG è neutrale rispetto allo schema da utilizzare. Lo schema di identificazione dovrebbe essere indicato dal datatype del valore dell'identificatore. L'uso del datatype per distinguere lo schema di identificazione è coerente con le best practice per \`[skos:notation](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#notation)\`, di cui questa proprietà è una specializzazione."@it
- <a id="change-719436286741300"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436286741300)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier): [rdfs:label](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label) = "identifiant"@fr
- <a id="change-719436286763900"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436286763900)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier): [rdfs:isDefinedBy](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#isDefinedBy) = "http://www.w3.org/ns/org"
- <a id="change-719436286825100"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436286825100)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Gives an identifier, such as a company registration number, that can be used to used to uniquely identify the organization. Many different national and international identier schemes are available. The org ontology is neutral to which schemes are used. The particular identifier scheme should be indicated by the datatype of the identifier value.  Using datatypes to distinguish the notation scheme used is consistent with recommended best practice for \`[skos:notation](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#notation)\` of which this property is a specialization."@en
- <a id="change-719436286893400"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436286893400)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#identifier): [rdfs:label](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label) = "tiene identificador"@es

It is composed of two values: (1)  line category, and (2) speed. Only line category is a list of predefined values (SKOS), whereas speed is an integer (according to the RINF application guide)."@en

  - Added annotation on [era:loadCapabilityLineCategory](http://data.europa.eu/949/loadCapabilityLineCategory): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Part of the load capability of a track that corresponds to the line category of the load model.  The load capability is a value selected from the list of load models representing: line category which is amended by value of speed [km/h] permitted for a specific load model. The list of values may also be Route Availability which is amended by value of speed [miles/h] permitted for a specific load model."@en

  - Added annotation on [era:opStart](http://data.europa.eu/949/opStart): [dcterms:modified](http://purl.org/dc/terms/modified) = "2024-01-08"^^[xsd:date](http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#date)
- <a id="change-719436240618700"></a>**[opStart](http://data.europa.eu/949/opStart)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436240618700)
  - Added annotation on [era:opStart](http://data.europa.eu/949/opStart): [rdfs:label](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label) = "Operational point at start of section of line"@en
- <a id="change-719436240677600"></a>**[opStart](http://data.europa.eu/949/opStart)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436240677600)
  - Added annotation on [era:opStart](http://data.europa.eu/949/opStart): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Unique OP ID at start of section of line (kilometres increasing from start OP to the end OP)."@en
- <a id="change-719436240783300"></a>**[opType](http://data.europa.eu/949/opType)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436240783300)
  - Added annotation on [era:opType](http://data.europa.eu/949/opType): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Type of facility in relation to the dominating operational functions."@en
- <a id="change-719436228291100"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436228291100)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Grupo de personas que se organiza en una comunidad u otro tipo de estructura social, comercial o política. Dicho grupo tiene un objetivo o motivo común para su existencia que va más allá del conjunto de personas que lo forman y que puede actuar como “agente”. A menudo las organizaciones se pueden agrupar en estructuras jerárquicas. Se recomienda el uso de etiquetas de SKOS para denominar a cada “organización”. En concreto, \`[skos:prefLabel](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#prefLabel)\` para la denominación principal o recomendada (aquella reconocida legalmente, siempre que sea posible), \`[skos:altLabel](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#altLabel)\` para denominaciones alternativas (nombre comercial, sigla, denominación por la que se conoce a la organización coloquialmente) y \`[skos:notation](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#notation)\` para referirse al código que identifique a la organización en una lista de códigos. Denominaciones alternativas: _colectivo_ _corporación_ _grupo_"@es
- <a id="change-719436228457200"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436228457200)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization): [rdfs:isDefinedBy](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#isDefinedBy) = "http://www.w3.org/ns/org"
- <a id="change-719436228514500"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436228514500)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Represents a collection of people organized together into a community or other social, commercial or political structure. The group has some common purpose or reason for existence which goes beyond the set of people belonging to it and can act as an Agent. Organizations are often decomposable into hierarchical structures.  It is recommended that SKOS lexical labels should be used to label the Organization. In particular \`[skos:prefLabel](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#prefLabel)\` for the primary (possibly legally recognized name), \`[skos:altLabel](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#altLabel)\` for alternative names (trading names, colloquial names) and \`[skos:notation](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#notation)\` to denote a code from a code list. Alternative names: _Collective_ _Body_ _Org_ _Group_"@en
- <a id="change-719436228582100"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436228582100)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization): [rdfs:label](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label) = "Organization"@en
- <a id="change-719436228658300"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436228658300)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Rappresenta una collezione di persone organizzate all'interno di una communità o di una qualche struttura sociale, commerciale o politica. Il gruppo condivide un obiettivo o una ragione d'essere che va oltre gli stessi membri appartenenti al gruppo e  può agire come un Agent. Le organizzazioni si possono spesso suddividere in strutture gerarchiche. Si raccomanda di usare le label per l'Organization mediante le proprietà di SKOS. In particolare, \`[skos:prefLabel](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#prefLabel)\` per il nome principale (possibilmente un nome legalmente riconosciuto)”, \`[skos:altLabel](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#altLabel)\` come nome alternativo (denominazione commerciale, denominazione colloquiale) e \`[skos:notation](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#notation)\` per indicare un codice di una lista di codici."@it
- <a id="change-719436228809500"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436228809500)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization): [rdfs:label](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label) = "organización"@es
- <a id="change-719436229117000"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436229117000)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "コミュニティー、その他の社会、商業、政治的な構造に共に編入された人々の集合を表わします。グループには、そこに属する人々を超えた、存在に対するある共通の目的や理由があり、エージェント（代理）を務めることができます。組織は、多くの場合、階層構造に分割できます。"@ja
- <a id="change-719436229217200"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436229217200)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Représente un groupe de personnes organisées en communauté où tout autre forme de structure sociale, commerciale ou politique. Le groupe a un but commun ou une raison d'être qui va au-delà de la somme des personnes qui en font partie et peut agir en tant que "Agent". Les organisations sont souvent décomposables en structures hiérarchisées. Il est recommandé que des labels lexicaux SKOS soient utilisés pour nommer l'Organisation. En particulier \`[skos:prefLabel](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#prefLabel)\` pour le nom principal (en général le nom légal), \`[skos:altLabel](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#altLabel)\` pour les noms alternatifs (marques, sigles, appellations familières) et \`[skos:notation](http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#notation)\` pour indiquer un code provenant d'une liste de code."@fr
- <a id="change-719436229291800"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436229291800)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization): [rdfs:label](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label) = "Organisation"@fr
- <a id="change-719436229351400"></a>**[<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436229351400)
  - Added annotation on [<http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization>](http://www.w3.org/ns/org#Organization): [rdfs:label](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label) = "Organizzazione"@it

According to the specification referenced in Appendix A-1, index [C]."@en

- <a id="change-719436243636100"></a>**[qNvdriverAdhes](http://data.europa.eu/949/qNvdriverAdhes)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436243636100)
  - Added annotation on [era:qNvdriverAdhes](http://data.europa.eu/949/qNvdriverAdhes): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Qualifier determining whether the driver is allowed to modify the adhesion factor used by the ETCS on-board to calculate the braking curves.  According to the specification referenced in Appendix A-1, index [C].  In https://www.era.europa.eu/sites/default/files/filesystem/ertms/ccs_tsi_annex_a_-_mandatory_specifications/set_of_specifications_3_etcs_b3_r2_gsm-r_b1/index004_-_subset-026_v360.zip  Subset 26, chapter 7. 7.5.1.122 Q_NVDRIVER_ADHES."@en
- <a id="change-719436243783300"></a>**[qNvemrrls](http://data.europa.eu/949/qNvemrrls)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436243783300)
  - Added annotation on [era:qNvemrrls](http://data.europa.eu/949/qNvemrrls): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Qualifier defining whether the application of the emergency brake for reasons other than a trip can be revoked as soon as the conditions for it have disappeared or after the train has come to a complete standstill. According to the specification referenced in Appendix A-1, index [C].  In https://www.era.europa.eu/sites/default/files/filesystem/ertms/ccs_tsi_annex_a_-_mandatory_specifications/set_of_specifications_3_etcs_b3_r2_gsm-r_b1/index004_-_subset-026_v360.zip Subset 26, chapter 7. 7.5.1.123 Q_NVEMRRLS."@en
- <a id="change-719436243937700"></a>**[quieterRoutesExemptedCountry](http://data.europa.eu/949/quieterRoutesExemptedCountry)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436243937700)

- <a id="change-719436244133400"></a>**[railInclination](http://data.europa.eu/949/railInclination)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436244133400)
  - Added annotation on [era:railInclination](http://data.europa.eu/949/railInclination): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "An angle defining the inclination of the head of a rail relative to the running surface."@en
- <a id="change-719436283307400"></a>**[raisedPantographsDistance](http://data.europa.eu/949/raisedPantographsDistance)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436283307400)

- <a id="change-719436227244600"></a>**[RaisedPantographsDistanceAndSpeed](http://data.europa.eu/949/RaisedPantographsDistanceAndSpeed)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436227244600)
  - Added annotation on [era:RaisedPantographsDistanceAndSpeed](http://data.europa.eu/949/RaisedPantographsDistanceAndSpeed): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Indication of maximum number of raised pantographs per train allowed and minimum spacing centre line to centre line of adjacent pantograph heads, expressed in metres, at the given speed. Each track can have several raised pantographs per train allowed (structured) values, and each one has values for number of pantographs, minimum distance between pantographs, in metres, and speed considered in km/h."@en
- <a id="change-719436283365100"></a>**[raisedPantographsDistanceAndSpeed](http://data.europa.eu/949/raisedPantographsDistanceAndSpeed)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436283365100)
  - Added annotation on [era:raisedPantographsDistanceAndSpeed](http://data.europa.eu/949/raisedPantographsDistanceAndSpeed): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Indication of maximum number of raised pantographs per train allowed and minimum spacing centre line to centre line of adjacent pantograph heads, expressed in metres, at the given speed. Deprecated because of replacement by a class and three integer properties The reason is that the property is composed of three values: [N] is number of pantographs; [NNN] is minimum distance between pantographs, in metres; [NNN] is the speed considered in km/h."@en
- <a id="change-719436283444900"></a>**[raisedPantographsNumber](http://data.europa.eu/949/raisedPantographsNumber)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-719436283444900)

  - Added annotation on [era:tsiCompliant](http://data.europa.eu/949/tsiCompliant): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Indicates if a certain property is TSI compliant.The reason for deprecation is that it is used in SKOS "OtherPantographHeads" and "TsiPantographHeads" to indicate in the first case if a SKOS concept is not tsiCompliant (false) and in the second case that the concept is tsiCompliant (true). Applicable if trackside HABD is not TSI compliant, direction of measurement of trackside Hot Axle Box Detector. If the direction of measurement is: —	the same as the direction defined by the start and end of the SoL: (N) —	the opposite to the direction defined by the start and end of the SoL: (O) —	both directions: (B). This property was previously a datatype property with domain Track and range string. As its values are a list of three predefined values, it is now a SKOS. The allowed values for this property belong to the SKOS Concept Scheme http://data.europa.eu/949/concepts/hot-axle/HotAxleBoxDetectorDirections"@en

- <a id="change-719436238782200"></a>**[loadCapability](http://data.europa.eu/949/loadCapability)** `Remove Annotation from Entity` [permalink](#change-719436238782200)
  - Removed annotation from [era:loadCapability](http://data.europa.eu/949/loadCapability): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "A combination of the line category and speed at the weakest point of the track. The allowed values for this property belong to the SKOS Concept Scheme http://data.europa.eu/949/concepts/load-capabilities/LoadCapabilities. The reason for deprecation is that the load capability property 1.1.1.1.2.4 was defined incorrectly as a SKOS property. It is composed of two values: (1)  line category, and (2) speed. Only line category is a list of predefined values (SKOS), whereas speed is an integer (according to the RINF application guide)."@en
- <a id="change-719436238890800"></a>**[loadCapabilityLineCategory](http://data.europa.eu/949/loadCapabilityLineCategory)** `Remove Annotation from Entity` [permalink](#change-719436238890800)
  - Removed annotation from [era:loadCapabilityLineCategory](http://data.europa.eu/949/loadCapabilityLineCategory): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "Part of the load capability of a track that corresponds to the line category of the load model.  The load capability is a value selected from the list of load models representing: line category which is amended by value of speed [km/h] permitted for a specific load model. The list of values may also be Route Availability which is amended by value of speed [miles/h] permitted for a specific load model. The allowed values for this property belong to the SKOS Concept Scheme http://data.europa.eu/949/concepts/load-capability-line-categories/LoadCapabilityLineCategories"@en

The allowed values for this property belong to the SKOS Concept Scheme http://data.europa.eu/949/concepts/magnetic-braking/MagneticBraking. This property has been modified to be a RINF parameter (its eratvIndex is deleted). It is treated as a SKOS by RINF and as a boolean by ERATV. A new ERATV datatype property has been defined, magneticBrakingFitted with boolean values. In 3.4.6 https://eur-lex.europa.eu/eli/reg_impl/2019/773/oj Permissibility to use magnetic brake."@en

- <a id="change-719436227130800"></a>**[RaisedPantographsDistanceAndSpeed](http://data.europa.eu/949/RaisedPantographsDistanceAndSpeed)** `Remove Annotation from Entity` [permalink](#change-719436227130800)

- <a id="change-719436283330200"></a>**[raisedPantographsDistanceAndSpeed](http://data.europa.eu/949/raisedPantographsDistanceAndSpeed)** `Remove Annotation from Entity` [permalink](#change-719436283330200)

Deprecated because of replacement by a class and three integer properties The reason is that the property is composed of three values: [N] is number of pantographs; [NNN] is minimum distance between pantographs, in metres; [NNN] is the speed considered in km/h."@en
